# Skills · L · part 52

1,031 entries, lmspeed to load-test-scenario-builder. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **lmspeed**: Select and benchmark fastest LLM models and inference providers based on LMSpeed real-time performance metrics (TPS throughput, TTFT latency, cost efficiency, and stream reliability). Use to pick the fastest endpoints for DeepSeek, Llama, Qwen, Claude, and GPT models, or optimize interactive agent speed. ([zedanazad43/zagros_kurdish_etymology_suite](https://github.com/zedanazad43/zagros_kurdish_etymology_suite/tree/HEAD/arsenal_and_skills_backup/skills/lmspeed))
- **lmstudio-api**: Справочник по HTTP-API LM Studio (сервер локальных моделей, обычно порт 1234). Используй когда надо программно управлять моделями на LM Studio - узнать что загружено, загрузить или выгрузить модель, задать длину контекста и параллельность, отключить размышления модели, - а также при диагностике: модель thinking/reasoning не отключается, enable_thinking игнорируется, ответ приходит пустым а весь бюджет уходит в reasoning_content, запросы падают с HTTP 500 на холодном старте или при загрузке крупной модели, ошибка Context size has been exceeded, Model is unloaded, эндпоинт отвечает 404 или 200… ([Desko77/claude-code-skills-1c](https://github.com/Desko77/claude-code-skills-1c/tree/HEAD/skills/lmstudio-api) · ★98 · 3 copies)
- **lmstudio-configuration**: Troubleshoot LM Studio model duplicates and Flatpak paths. ([mghasemi/AgentDesign](https://github.com/mghasemi/AgentDesign/tree/HEAD/librarian/skills/mlops/lmstudio-configuration) · 2 copies)
- **lmstudio-hermes-local-model**: Use when configuring LM Studio local models in Hermes. ([Madmasx/hermes-backup](https://github.com/Madmasx/hermes-backup/tree/HEAD/.hermes/skills/mlops-inference/lmstudio-hermes-local-model))
- **lmstudio-model-hub**: Register a Hugging Face GGUF model in LM Studio's hub so it gets a clean `publisher/model-name` identifier in the `/v1/models` API. Use this skill when the user provides a HF repo (e.g. `unsloth/Qwen3.6-27B-GGUF`) and wants it properly registered with the original model author as publisher. Also use when the user mentions LM Studio model IDs lacking publisher names, or wants to clean up their LM Studio model list. ([Vigilans/agents](https://github.com/Vigilans/agents/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/lmstudio-model-hub))
- **lmstudio-subagents**: Reduces token usage from paid providers by offloading work to local LM Studio models. Use when: (1) Cutting costs—use local models for summarization, extraction, classification, rewriting, first-pass review, brainstorming when quality suffices, (2) Avoiding paid API calls for high-volume or repetitive tasks, (3) No extra model configuration—JIT loading and REST API work with existing LM Studio setup, (4) Local-only or privacy-sensitive work. Requires LM Studio 0.4+ with server (default :1234). No CLI required. ([sundial-org/awesome-openclaw-skills](https://github.com/sundial-org/awesome-openclaw-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lmstudio-subagents) · ★677 · 8 copies)
- **lmux**: Drive the lmux terminal from inside it - open a workspace running another agent (Codex, Claude Code, any CLI), wait for it to finish or need the user, and read its result. Use when running in an lmux pane ($LMUX_PANE_ID is set) and the user asks to hand work to another agent, run something in a new workspace, or wait for another pane. ([sooftware/lmux](https://github.com/sooftware/lmux/tree/HEAD/skills/lmux) · ★36)
- **lmux**: Control tmux panes and communicate between AI agents on this machine. Use whenever the user mentions tmux panes or windows, cross-pane communication, sending work to or reading from another agent, spawning panes for parallel work, or driving a process running in another pane. Provides the lmux CLI plus raw tmux commands. ([lukemiloszewski/dotfiles](https://github.com/lukemiloszewski/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/plugins/lmux/skills/lmux))
